# Skills · E · part 69

846 entries, explain-candidate to explainer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **explain-candidate**: Explain one SetuHQ transit candidate in plain words from its stored record, with every reviewer's verdict, the vetting flags, the transit fit, the false-alarm probability and the full provenance. - **explain-cdr-error**: Translate a raw CDR error (SDK class name, error code, viem revert string, or stack trace) into the root cause + the fix. ([Blockchain-Oracle/cdr-kit](https://github.com/Blockchain-Oracle/cdr-kit/tree/HEAD/skills/explain-cdr-error))
